## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/11 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓ वा᳓ एते᳓ कृष्णाजिने᳓ प्रा᳓ग्ग्रीवे उ᳓पस्तृणाति [[तयोरेनम᳓धि|त᳓योरेनम᳓धि]] दीक्षयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He spreads these two black antelope-skins, with their neck-parts towards the east. On them does he consecrate this (sacrificer).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते वा᳘ एते᳘ कृष्णाजिने प्रा᳘ग्ग्रीवे उ᳘पस्तृणाति तयोरेनम᳘धि दीक्षयति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते य᳓दि द्वे᳓ भ᳓वतोऽन᳓योरेवै᳙नं त᳓ल्लोक᳓योर᳓धि दीक्षयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If they are two, he verily consecrates him thus on these two worlds (heaven and earth).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते य᳘दि द्वे भ᳘वतोऽन᳘योरे᳘वैनं त᳘ल्लोक᳘योर᳘धि दीक्षयति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते सं᳓बद्धान्ते स्यातां सं᳓बद्धान्ताविव ही᳙मौ᳓ लोकौ᳓ तर्द्मसमुते᳓ पश्चा᳓त्स्यातामिमा᳓वे᳓वैत᳓ल्लोकौ᳓ मिथुनीकृ᳓त्य त᳓योरेनम᳓धि दीक्षयति त᳓स्मात्तर्द्मसमुते᳓ [[पश्चात्स्याताम्|पश्चा᳓त्स्याताम्]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Those two should be such that their edges are well connected—these two worlds are, as it were, with their edges well connected—and at the hind part they should be well fastened together through holes. Thus having made these two worlds into a pair, on them does he consecrate this (sacrificer). Therefore those two (skins) should be in the hind part fastened together through holes.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते सं᳘बद्धान्ते स्याताꣳ सं᳘बद्धान्ताविव᳘ हीमौ᳘ लोकौ᳘ तर्द्मसमुते᳘ पश्चा᳘त्स्यातामिमावे᳘वैत᳘ल्लोकौ᳘ मिथुनीकृ᳘त्य त᳘योरेनम᳘धि दीक्षयति त᳘स्मात्तर्द्मसमुते᳘ पश्चात्स्याताम् ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/46 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓द्युवे᳓कं भ᳓वत्येषा᳓मेव᳓ त᳓ल्लोका᳓नां रूपं᳓ या᳓नि [[शुक्लानि|शुक्ला᳓नि]] ता᳓न्यस्या᳓ या᳓नि कृष्णा᳓नि ता᳓नि दिवो᳓ य᳓दि वेतर᳓था या᳓नि कृष्णा᳓नि ता᳓न्यस्या᳓ या᳓नि शुक्ला᳓नि ता᳓नि दिवो᳓ऽथ या᳓न्येव᳓ म᳓ध्ये बभ्रू᳓णि वा ह᳓रिणि वा [[ता᳓न्यन्तरिक्षस्य|ता᳓न्यन्त᳓रिक्षस्य]] त᳓द्वेनं᳓ [[तदेष्वे᳙व᳓|त᳓देष्वे᳙व᳓]] लोके᳓ष्व᳓धि दीक्षयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

But if there is only one (skin), that is the form of these very worlds (the three worlds). Those (hairs on the skin) which are white they are the form of this (earth); those which are black—they are (the form) of heaven. Or (it may be) otherwise. Those which are black are (of the form) of this (earth). Those which are white, they are of (the form of) heaven. Those which are in the middle, tawny or yellow, they are (the form) of the mid-region. Thus he consecrates this person (the sacrificer) on these (three) worlds indeed. Then he tucks (folds) the hind and underneath.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘द्युवे᳘कं भ᳘वत्येषा᳘मेव त᳘ल्लोका᳘नाꣳ रूपं या᳘नि शुक्लानि ता᳘न्यस्या या᳘नि कृष्णा᳘नि ता᳘नि दिवो य᳘दि वेतर᳘था या᳘नि कृष्णा᳘नि ता᳘न्यस्या या᳘नि शुक्ला᳘नि ता᳘नि दिवो᳘ऽथ या᳘न्येव म᳘ध्ये बभ्रू᳘णि वा ह᳘रिणि वा ता᳘न्यन्तरिक्षस्य त᳘द्वेनं तदे᳘ष्वेव᳘ लोकेष्व᳘धि दीक्षयति
</details>

ग᳓र्भो वा᳓ एष᳓ [[भूत᳓श्छन्दांसि|भूत᳓श्छ᳓न्दांसि]] प्र᳓विशति यो᳓ दी᳓क्षते ।

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He verily says (means) "You two are replicas of 'Ṛks' and 'Sāmans'. (He chants) "Such as you two are, I take hold of you.
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/9 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्मात्स᳓मक्णाङ्गुलिरिव भवति स᳓मक्णाङ्गुलिरिव ह्य᳙यं᳓ [[गर्भः|ग᳓र्भः]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Such as you are, you two please protect me". He verily says (means) this, "I enter you two". When he says, "I take hold of you two, such as you are". (In saying) "You two, as you are such, please protect me". (In saying) "Till the end of this sacrifice", he merely says (means) this, "upto the completion of this sacrifice". This person who undertakes the consecration for the vow indeed enters into the metres, having become an embryo. Therefore he is as if with closed fingers, for the embryo has its fingers closed (curled up) as it were.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्मात्स᳘मक्णाङ्गुलिरिव भवति स᳘मक्णाङ्गुलिरिव᳘ ह्ययं गर्भः ॥३॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/16 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थोरु᳓णा᳓धिसर्पति श᳓र्मासि श᳓र्म मे यच्छ न᳓मस्ते [[अस्तु᳓|अस्तु]] मा᳓ मा हिंसी᳓रि᳓ति ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he mounts on it, on his thighs, (chanting), "You are the refuge. Give me happiness. May Obeisance be to you! Do not hurt me".
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

कृ᳓ष्णस्य वा᳓ एत᳓च्च᳓र्म [[त᳓दस्यैतन्मानुषं᳓|त᳓दस्यैत᳓न्मानुषं᳓]] ना᳓म श᳓र्म देवत्रा᳓ स᳓ य᳓देव᳓ देवत्रा᳓ त᳓त्करो᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This is indeed the skin (carman) of the black antelope. This is its name (Carman) among men. Among gods it is śarman (happiness). What is prevalent among the gods that verily he adopts. Therefore he says, "You are the refuge; give me happiness".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

कृ᳘ष्णस्य वा᳘ एतच्च᳘र्म त᳘दस्यैतन्मानुषं ना᳘म श᳘र्म देवत्रा स य᳘देव᳘ देवत्रा त᳘त्करो᳘ति
</details>

## ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/35 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ नि᳓रन्त [[हवैवा᳓ग्र|ह᳓वैवा᳓ग्र]] आसीत य᳓द्ध प्रथमे᳓नैव᳓ म᳓ध्यं प्रविशे᳓द्यो᳓ हैनं [[त᳓त्रानुव्याहरेद्द्रप्स्य᳓ति|त᳓त्रानुव्याह᳓रेद्द्रप्स्य᳓ति]] वा मरिष्य᳓ति वा प्र᳓ वा पतिष्यतीती᳙श्वरो᳓ ह त᳓थैव᳓ स्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

First he should sit at the very end (of the skin). If he should enter the mid part of the skin even at first, whoever might curse him (saying) "Hewill fall (because of pride)" or "He will die" or "He will fall absolutely", it could indeed happen like that.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स नि᳘रन्त हवैवा᳘ग्र आसीत य᳘द्ध प्रथमे᳘नैव म᳘ध्यं प्रविशेद्यो᳘ हैनं त᳘त्रानुव्याहरेद्द्रप्स्य᳘ति वा मरिष्य᳘ति वा प्र᳘ वा पतिष्यती᳘तीश्वरो᳘ ह त᳘थैव᳘ स्यात्
</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ य᳓न्मे᳓खलां परिह᳓रते य᳓त्र ह वा᳓ [[अङ्गिरसो|अ᳓ङ्गिरसो]] दीक्षिता᳓ अबल्यं᳙ निजग्मु᳓स्त᳓द्घो᳓पाजिज्ञासन्त ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(One reason) why he wears the girdle around (his waist) is (this): When the Angirasas were consecrated (for the vow), they attained debility. Then they wanted to know, "How indeed may we get (strength)?" They did not find any food other than (what was allowed for) the vow.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स यन्मे᳘खलां परिह᳘रते य᳘त्र ह वा अङ्गिरसो दीक्षिता᳘ अब᳘ल्यं निजग्मुस्तद्घो᳘पाजिज्ञासन्त
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/41 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

कथं᳓ नु᳓ स᳓माप्नुयामे᳓ति [[ते᳓ना᳓हान्य᳓द्व्रता᳓द᳓शनमुपा᳓जानंस्त|ते᳓ना᳓हान्य᳓द्व्रता᳓द᳓शनमु᳓पाजानंस्त᳓]] एता᳓मेवो᳓र्जमपश्यन्नेतां᳓ मे᳓खलां तां᳓ मध्यतो᳙ऽदधत स᳓माप्तये त᳓या स᳓माप्नुवंस्त᳓थो वा᳓ एष᳓ एता᳓मू᳓र्जं मध्यतो᳓ धत्ते स᳓माप्तये त᳓या स᳓माप्नोति त᳓स्मान्मे᳓खलां प᳓रिहरते ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They found only this (means of) strength viz., this girdle. They wore it at the waist for well acquiring (strength). By means of that they got (strength). Therefore is it that this (sacrificer) wears this (means of) (strength) thus at the waist for the sake of acquisition (of strength). He acquires (strength) by that. Therefore he wears the girdle.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

कथं नु स᳘माप्नुयामे᳘ति तेना᳘हान्य᳘द्व्रताद᳘शनमुपा᳘जानꣳस्त एता᳘मेवो᳘र्जमपश्यन्नेतां मे᳘खलां तां᳘ मध्य᳘तोऽदधत स᳘माप्तये त᳘या स᳘माप्नुवꣳस्त᳘थो वा᳘ एष᳘ एतामू᳘र्जं मध्यतो᳘ धत्ते स᳘माप्तये त᳘या स᳘माप्नोति त᳘स्मान्मे᳘खलां प᳘रिहरते ॥६॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्य ह य᳓न्ने᳓दिष्ठमु᳓ल्बमा᳓स ते᳓ शणा᳓स्त᳓स्माच्छणा᳓ दुर्गन्ध᳓यो वा᳓न्त्य᳓थ य᳓द्बा᳓ह्यं जरा᳓यु त᳓द्दीक्षितव᳓सनं य᳓तो वै᳓ त᳓त्प्रजा᳓पतिर᳓तः प्रथमं᳓ ग᳓र्भो भूतो᳓ऽजायत ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore is it made of hemp. Now, when Prajāpati, first having become embryo was born, what was nearest him, viz., the amnion, was hemp fibres. Therefore the hemp plants are foul-smelling. And what was the outer (membrane), the placenta, that became the garment of the consecrated person. As Prajāpati, first having become an embryo, was born, in the same manner indeed is this person (the sacrificer) born, first having become, an embryo.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्य ह यन्ने᳘दिष्ठमु᳘ल्बमा᳘स ते᳘ शणास्त᳘स्माच्छणा᳘ दुर्गन्ध᳘यो वान्त्य᳘थ यद्बा᳘ह्यं जरा᳘यु त᳘द्दीक्षितव᳘सनं य᳘तो वै त᳘त्प्रजा᳘पतिर᳘तः प्रथमं ग᳘र्भो भूतो᳘ऽजायत
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

एव᳓मु वा᳓ एष᳓ एत᳓द᳓तः प्रथमं᳓ ग᳓र्भो [[भूतो|भूतो᳓]] जायते त᳓स्माच्छाणी᳓ मे᳓खला᳓न्तरा दीक्षितव᳓सनाद्भवत्य᳓न्तरं हो᳓ल्बं जरा᳓युणो [[भ᳓वति|भवति]] त᳓स्माच्छाणी᳓ भवति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore the hempen girdle is (worn) within the garment of the consecrated person; for, the amnion is under the placenta (the outer-membrane). Therefore it (the girdle) is made of hemp.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एव᳘मु वा᳘ एष᳘ एतद᳘तः प्रथमं ग᳘र्भो भूतो जायते त᳘स्माच्छाणी मे᳘खला᳘न्तरा दीक्षितव᳘सनाद्भवत्य᳘न्तरꣳ हु᳘ल्बं जरा᳘युणो भ᳘वति त᳘स्माच्छाणी᳘ भवति ॥७॥
</details>

## ०८

</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

It is triple (of three rounds), for, indeed, food is three-fold.

## ०९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/12 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्यां [[मुञ्जवल्शो᳙ऽन्व᳓स्तो|मुञ्जवल्शो᳓ऽन्वस्तो]] भवति व᳓ज्रो वै᳓ शरो᳓ विरक्ष᳓स्तायै सा᳓ स्तुकास᳓र्गं सृष्टा᳓ भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

A 'muñja' (reed) shoot is placed within that—indeed the reed is thunderbolt—for driving away the evil spirits. It is made (twisted) in the manner of a braid (or tuft) of hair.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्यां मुञ्जवल्शो᳘ऽन्व᳘स्तो भवति व᳘ज्रो वै᳘ शरो᳘ विरक्ष᳘स्तायै सा᳘ स्तुकास᳘र्गꣳ सृष्टा᳘ भवति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓दु हापसलवि᳓ सृष्टा᳓ स्या᳓त्पितृदेवत्या᳙ स्याद᳓थैवं᳓ देवत्रा᳓ [[त᳓स्मात्स्तुकासर्गं|त᳓स्मात्स्तुकास᳓र्गं]] [[सृष्टा|सृष्टा᳓]] भवति ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/34 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

तां᳓ प᳓रिहरत [[ऊर्गस्याङ्गिरसी᳓त्य᳓ङ्गिरसो|ऊ᳓र्गस्याङ्गिरसी᳓त्य᳓ङ्गिरसो]] ह्ये᳙ता᳓मू᳓र्जम᳓पश्यंस्त᳓स्मादाहाङ्गिरसी᳓त्यू᳓र्णम्रदा इ᳓त्यू᳓र्णामृद्वी᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाहो᳓र्जं मे यच्छे᳓त्यू᳓र्जं मे यच्छे᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He girds it around (his waist) chanting, "You are the strength of Angiras". Indeed the 'Angirasas' discovered this (means of) strength. Therefore does he say, "of Angiras". (He says) "wool-soft". He verily says (means) only "soft like wool". (He says) "Bestow strength on me". He verily says this, "Give me strength".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तां प᳘रिहरत ऊर्गस्याङ्गिरसीत्य᳘ङ्गिरसो᳘ ह्येतामू᳘र्जम᳘पश्यꣳस्त᳘स्मादाहाङ्गिरसीत्यू᳘र्णम्रदा इत्यू᳘र्णामृद्वी᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाहो᳘र्जं मे यच्छेत्यू᳘र्जं मे यच्छे᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह ॥१०॥
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/34 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/26 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ नीविं᳓ कुरुते सो᳓मस्य नीवि᳓रसी᳓ति मानुषो᳓ वा᳓ अस्यै᳓षा᳓ग्रे नीवि᳓र्भवत्य᳓थैषा᳓ सो᳓मस्य नीवि᳓र्य᳓द्दीक्षित᳓स्य त᳓स्मादाह᳓ [[सोमस्य|सो᳓मस्य]] [[नीवि᳓रसीति|नीवि᳓रसी᳓ति]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he tucks up (inserts) the end of his (nether) garment, (chanting) "You are the tuck of Soma". First his (the sacrificer's) tuck (before consecration) is indeed of a (mere) human. Then this tuck of the consecrated person becomes the tuck of Soma. Therefore says he, "You are Soma's tuck".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ नीविं᳘ कुरुते सो᳘मस्य नीवि᳘रसी᳘ति मानुषो वा᳘ अस्यैषा᳘ग्रे नीवि᳘र्भवत्य᳘थैषा सो᳘मस्य नीविर्य᳘द्दीक्षित᳘स्य त᳘स्मादाह सोमस्य नीवि᳘रसीति ॥११॥
</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/10 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थैनं प्रो᳓र्णोति ग᳓र्भो वा᳓ एष᳓ भवति यो᳓ [[दीक्षते|दी᳓क्षते]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he covers him (the consecrated person)—Indeed this person who undertakes the vow becomes an embryo.
</details>

अ᳘थैनं प्रो᳘र्णोति ग᳘र्भो वा᳘ एष᳘ भवति यो दीक्षते

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ प्रो᳓र्णोति वि᳓ष्णोः श᳓र्मासि श᳓र्म य᳓जमानस्ये᳓ति ।

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अमूं᳓ वै᳓ त᳓द्देवा᳓ दि᳓वमु᳓पा᳓यन्निमा᳓म᳓सुराः स᳓ हायं᳓ यज्ञो᳓ देवै᳓र्वोक्तः᳓ स्वयं᳓ वा दध्रे ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

the gods verily (got) the sacrifice and the Asuras (got) speech. Thus the gods indeed got the mind, the Asuras the speech. The gods then got the yonder Heaven, and the Asuras this (Earth). This 'Yajña' was either, told by the gods or 'he' thought by himself, "Well! Let me solicit this 'Vāk'. Or perhaps the gods said to him, "This 'Vāk' is a female.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/13 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

तां᳓ होपमन्त्रयां᳓ चक्रे᳓ [[सा|सा᳓]] हास्मै दूर᳓मिवैवा᳓ग्रेऽसूयां᳓ चकार ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He solicited her, indeed. At first she disdained him afar.

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For gods it is an offering (huta) (that is made). This 'Vāk', which is such, was bound—which formerly one offers with (a chant in) Anuṣṭubh (metre) in the 'audgrabhanas' (chants that raise one up)—That is the 'Vāk' by binding.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

हुतꣳ हि देवा᳘ना᳘ꣳ᳘ सैषा वा᳘ङ्निदा᳘नेन या᳘मद᳘ औद्ग्रभणे᳘ष्वनुष्टु᳘भा जुहोत्येषा सा वाङ्निदा᳘नेन ॥१७॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[स|स᳓]] [[म्ले᳓च्छस्त᳓स्माद्ब्राह्मणे᳓न|म्लेच्छ᳓स्त᳓स्माद्ब्राह्मणे᳓न]] न᳓ म्लेच्छित᳓व्यमसुर्या᳙ ह्ये᳙षा᳓ वा᳓गेवं᳓ ह वाव᳓ द्विषतः᳓ सप᳓त्नस्य वा᳓चमा᳓दत्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (who utters such speech) is a 'mleccha' (a barbarian). Therefore a Brāhmaṇa should not behave barbarously (in speech). For this (barbarous) 'vāk' belongs to the Asuras. He who knows thus—he thus takes up, indeed, the speech of the hateful rival and his rivals, their speech taken away, are defeated.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स म्ले᳘च्छस्त᳘स्माद्ब्राह्मणे᳘न न᳘ म्लेच्छित᳘व्यमसु᳘र्या᳘ ह्येषा वा᳘गेव᳘ꣳ᳘ ह वाव᳘ द्विषतः᳘ सप᳘त्नस्य वा᳘चमा᳘दत्ते
</details>

## १९

[[त᳓दुभयं|त᳓दुभ᳓यं]] देवे᳓ष्वासीद्वा᳓क्च यज्ञ᳓श्च स᳓ हायं᳓ यज्ञ᳓ इमां᳓ वा᳓चमभि᳓दध्यौ मिथुन्ये᳙नां स्या᳓मि᳓ति तां᳓ सं᳓बभूव त᳓स्यां रे᳓तः प्र᳓सिषेच ।

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सा᳓ कृष्णविषाणा᳓ त᳓देषो᳓ह स᳓ यज्ञो᳓ य᳓त्कृष्णाजिन᳓मेषो᳙ सा᳓ यो᳓निर्य᳓त्कृष्णविषाणा᳓ य᳓थो वै᳓ त᳓दि᳓न्द्र एत᳓न्मिथुनं᳓ ग᳓र्भो भूत्वा᳓ प्रा᳓विशत्स᳓ एत᳓स्मान्मिथुना᳓द᳓जायत ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That it is verily this thing viz., the black antelope skin that is the sacrifice. This thing viz., the horn of the black antelope is indeed that womb. Just as then Indra, having become an embryo, entered this union (pair) and was born from this union (pair), in this same manner indeed does this (sacrificer) enter this union, having become an embryo and he is born of this union.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सा᳘ कृष्णविषाणा त᳘देषो᳘ह स᳘ यज्ञो य᳘त्कृष्णाजिन᳘मे᳘षो सा यो᳘निर्य᳘त्कृष्णविषाणा य᳘थो वै तदि᳘न्द्र एत᳘न्मिथुनं ग᳘र्भो भूत्वा प्रा᳘विशत्स᳘ एत᳘स्मान्मिथुनाद᳘जायत
</details>

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ शिरस्त᳓ उ᳓पस्पृशती᳓न्द्रस्य यो᳓निरसी᳓ती᳓न्द्रस्य ह्ये᳙षा᳓ [[यो᳓निरेतस्या|यो᳓निरेत᳓स्या]] उ ही᳓न्द्रो᳓ऽजायत त᳓स्मादाहे᳓न्द्रस्य यो᳓निरसी᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He touches it with the head (chanting), "You are the womb (source) of Indra". Indeed, is this the source (womb) of Indra, for verily from this was Indra born.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ शिरस्त उ᳘पस्पृशती᳘न्द्रस्य यो᳘निरसीती᳘न्द्रस्य᳘ ह्येषा यो᳘निरेतस्या उ हीन्द्रो᳘ऽजायत त᳘स्मादाहे᳘न्द्रस्य यो᳘निरसी᳘ति
</details>

स᳓ य᳓च्छिरस्त᳓ उपस्पृश᳓त्य᳓तो वा᳓ एनामेत᳓द᳓ग्रे प्रविश᳓न्प्रविश᳓त्य᳓तो वा᳓ अ᳓ग्रे जा᳓यमानो जायते त᳓स्माच्छिरस्त᳓ उ᳓पस्पृशति [[सुसस्याः|सुसस्याः᳓]] कृषी᳓स्कृधी᳓ति [[भूमेरु᳓ल्लिखति|भू᳓मेरु᳓ल्लिखति]] यज्ञ᳓मेवै᳙त᳓ज्जनयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/9 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

यदा᳓ [[हि|हि᳓]] सु᳓प᳓क्त्यो᳓षधयः पच्य᳓न्ते᳓ऽथ हि᳓ य᳓जन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore he says, "You are Indra's womb (source). (The reason) why he touches with the head is that therewith he enters it (the womb) first entering thus. And he who is born is indeed born therewith (with the head) first. Therefore he touches it with the head. He scratches (draws a line on) the earth (with the horn), chanting, "Make the ploughed grounds have (plentiful) good crops". Thus he produces the sacrifice itself. When the plants ripen (grow) with good ripening, then they perform sacrifice. In a bad year they are not capable of (taking care of) themselves. Therefore he says, "Make the tilled earth have good crops".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यदा हि᳘ सुपक्त्यो᳘षधयः पच्यन्ते᳘ऽथ हि य᳘जन्ते
</details>

## २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ दीक्षितो᳓ न᳓ नखे᳓न न᳓ काष्ठे᳓न कण्डूयेत ग᳓र्भो वा᳓ एष᳓ भवति यो᳓ दी᳓क्षते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He who has undertaken the vow (and consecrated himself) shall not scratch (himself) with the nail or with ( a piece of) wood. For he who has undertaken the vow becomes an embryo.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ दीक्षितो न᳘ नखे᳘न न᳘ काष्ठे᳘न कण्डूयेत ग᳘र्भो वा᳘ एष᳘ भवति यो दी᳘क्षते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓त्र वै᳓ ग᳓र्भस्य नखे᳓न वा काष्ठे᳓न वा कण्डूये᳓द᳓पमृत्येदस्य᳓ [[त᳓त्स|त᳓त्स᳓]] हेश्वरो᳓ दीक्षितः᳓ पामनो᳓ भ᳓वितोरेत᳓मु वा᳓ अ᳓नु रे᳓तांसि ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he would scratch the embryo with the nail or (a piece of) wood, that would be its untimely death. Thereby the consecrated person (dīkṣita) could become scabby.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘त्र वै ग᳘र्भस्य नखे᳘न वा काष्ठे᳘न वा कण्डूयेद᳘पमृत्येदस्य तत्स हे श्वरो᳘ दीक्षितः᳘ पामनो भ᳘वितोरेत᳘मु वा अ᳘नु रे᳘ताꣳसि
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓ हेश्वरो᳓ ग᳓र्भाः पामना᳓ ज᳓नितोस्त᳓स्मादेत᳓यैव᳓ कण्डूयेत ।

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed the seeds (offspring) follow him. Those (future) embryos (children) could be born scabby. Therefore he scratches (himself) only with this (antelope horn).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘ हेश्वरो ग᳘र्भाः पामना ज᳘नितोस्त᳘स्मादेत᳘यैव᳘ कण्डूयेत

## २३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/39 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थास्मा᳓ [[औदुम्बरं|औ᳓दुम्बरं]] प्र᳓यच्छन्ति प्रतिल᳓म्भायैवौ᳓दुम्बर इ᳓ति होवाच [[याज्ञवल्क्यो|या᳓ज्ञवल्क्यो]] ने᳓दौपस᳓दोऽतिवर्ता᳓ इ᳓त्य᳓थो व᳓ज्रो वै᳓ दण्डो᳓ विरक्ष᳓स्ताया औ᳓दुम्बरो भवत्य᳓न्नं वा᳓ ऊ᳓र्गुदुम्ब᳓रस्त᳓स्मादौ᳓दुम्बरो भवति [[मु᳓खसंमितो|मु᳓खसंमितो᳓]] भवति [[मु᳓खसंमितं᳓|मु᳓खसं᳓मितं]] ही᳙दं᳓ वीर्य᳙म् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he (the Adhvaryu) gives to him ( a staff) of ‘Udumbara’ (ficus Glomerata) tree. Yājñavalkya said, “The Udumbara (staff) is verily for holding (for support). If not one, the ‘upasād’ ritual may transgress”. The staff is really the thunderbolt, for driving away evil forces. It is of the Udumbara tree. Udumbara is food, strength. Therefore is it (the staff) made of ‘Udumbara’ wood. It is upto the mouth (measured up to the mouth) (in length). For this strength is upto the mouth.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थास्मा औदुम्बरं प्र᳘यच्छन्ति प्रतिल᳘म्भायैवौ᳘दुम्बर इ᳘ति होवाच याज्ञवल्क्यो ने᳘दौपस᳘दोऽतिवर्ता इत्य᳘थो व᳘ज्रो वै᳘ दण्डो᳘ विरक्ष᳘स्ताया औ᳘दुम्बरो भवत्य᳘न्नं वा ऊ᳘र्गुदुम्ब᳘रस्त᳘स्मादौ᳘दुम्बरो भवति मु᳘खसंमितो भवति मु᳘खसंमित᳘ꣳ᳘ हीदं᳘ वी᳘र्यम् ॥२३॥
</details>

## २४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ प्र᳓यच्छत्यु᳓च्छ्रयस्व वनस्पत ऊर्ध्वो᳓ मा पाह्यं᳓हसः ।  
आ᳓स्य᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्योदृ᳓च इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He gives (the staff to the sacrificer) (chanting) “Oh tree! Rise tall, erect, save me from sin (harm); till the completion of this sacrifice”.
</details>

## २५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓त्र है᳓केऽङ्गु᳓लीर्न्य᳙च᳓यन्त्य᳓त्र वा᳓चं यमयन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Here (now) indeed, some bend their fingers and now they restrain their speech (thinking), “Hereafter only he is not going to mutter “Yajus” (chant) and he is not going to talk irrelevantly from now on till the end of the sacrifice and he commences (obtains) the sacrifice”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘त्र है᳘केऽङ्गु᳘लीर्न्यच᳘यन्त्य᳘त्र वा᳘चं यमयन्ति
</details>

## २६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓ममु᳓त्राङ्गु᳓लीर्न्य᳙च᳓यन्त्यमु᳓त्र वा᳓चं यम᳓यन्ती᳓त्युता᳙स्य᳓ य᳓जुर्वा ज᳓पतोऽन्यां᳓ वा वा᳓चमपव्या᳓ह᳓रतो यज्ञः᳓ प्र᳓स्रंसत इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

What they do formerly in that they make him (the sacrificer) bend his fingers and control his speech, saying, “The sacrifice of that person, who either mutters a yajus or says any other thing irrelevantly, disintegrates”—one need not do so (here).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व᳓दन्तस्त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ कुर्याद्य᳓था यं᳓ जि᳓घृक्षेत्त᳓मतिसृ᳓ज्यानुलि᳓प्समानो ना᳙नुल᳓भेत ना᳙न्वाप्नुया᳓त् ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

यज्ञो᳓ वै᳓ य᳓जुर्वा᳓हुतिर्वाभिस्थिर᳓मभिस्थिरमेव᳓ [[तद्यज्ञ᳓मा᳓रभते|त᳓द्यज्ञ᳓मा᳓रभते]] य᳓द्यु पुरा᳓ मानुषीं᳓ का᳓ञ्चिद्वा᳓चमपव्याह᳓रेद्वैष्णवं᳓ य᳓जुर्वा᳓चं वा जपेद्यज्ञो᳓ वै᳓ वि᳓ष्णुस्त᳓त्पु᳓नर्यज्ञमा᳓रभते ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore only formerly he may bend his fingers and on that occasion restrain his speech. Thus he obtains the sacrifice, just as one who may wish to grab a person may secure him by getting up towards him as he comes. (The reason) why he mutters ‘Yajus’ then or pours the oblation (into the fire) is that, indeed, ‘yajus’ or the oblation is the sacrifice. Thus he obtains (commences) a very firm and a very stable sacrifice. If formerly he should have uttered any human irrelevant speech, then he should mutter a ‘Yajus’ related to Viṣṇu or Ṛk (related to Viṣṇu). For Viṣṇu indeed is the sacrifice. Thus he gets back the sacrifice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यज्ञो वै य᳘जुर्वा᳘हुतिर्वाभिस्थिर᳘मभिस्थिरमेव तद्यज्ञमा᳘रभते य᳘द्यु पुरा᳘ मानुषीं का᳘ञ्चिद्वा᳘चमपव्याह᳘रेद्वैष्णवं य᳘जुर्वा᳘चं वा जपेद्यज्ञो वै वि᳘ष्णुस्तत्पु᳘नर्यज्ञमा᳘रभते ॥२६॥
</details>

## २७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थैनं प्रा᳓हुर᳓दीक्षिष्टायं᳓ ब्राह्मण᳓ इ᳓ति नि᳓वेदितमेवै᳙नमेत᳓त्स᳓न्तं देवे᳓भ्यो भू᳓यो नि᳓वेदयत्ययं᳓ यौष्मा᳓क [[इ᳓तीमं|इ᳓तीमं᳓]] गोपायतेमं᳓ वित्ते᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then they say of him, “This Brāhmaṇa has taken the vow (has been consecrated). Him who has been thus announced, he (the Adhvaryu) announces to the gods again, “This person belongs to you”. “Protect this person”. “Know this person”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थैनं प्रा᳘हुर᳘दीक्षिष्टायं᳘ ब्राह्मण इ᳘ति नि᳘वेदितमे᳘वैनमेतत्स᳘न्तं देवे᳘भ्यो भू᳘यो नि᳘वेदयत्ययं᳘ यौष्मा᳘क इ᳘तीमं गोपायतेमं᳘ वित्ते᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/68 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

देवा᳓न्ह्ये᳙ष᳓ उपाव᳓र्तते यो᳓ दी᳓क्षते स᳓ देव᳓तानामेवै᳓को भवत्य᳓थ य᳓दा᳓ह ब्राह्मण᳓ इ᳓त्येत᳓र्हि वा᳓ एष᳓ ब्र᳓ह्मणो जायते यो᳓ यज्ञा᳓च्छ᳓न्दोभ्यः श्लेष्म᳓णो वा ह्य᳓ग्रे जा᳓यते य᳓स्माद्वा को᳓ हि᳓ त᳓द्वे᳓द य᳓द्य᳓पि स्त्रि᳓यं र᳓क्षांस्यभिस᳓चन्ते᳓ऽथैत᳓र्हि ब्र᳓ह्मणो [[जा᳓यते|जायते]] यो᳓ [[यज्ञा᳓च्छ᳓न्दोभ्यस्त᳓स्माद्यद्य᳓प्य᳓ब्राह्मणो|यज्ञा᳓च्छ᳓न्दोभ्यस्त᳓स्माद्य᳓द्य᳓प्य᳓ब्राह्मणो]] दी᳓क्षते राजन्यो᳙ वा वै᳓श्यो वा ब्राह्मण᳓ इ᳓त्ये᳓वैनमाहुरेत᳓र्हि हि᳓ ब्र᳓ह्मणो जा᳓यते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For, he who undertakes the vow (and is consecrated) indeed goes to the gods; he becomes verily one of the gods themsleves. Why he says, “Brāhmaņa” (is this): This person who is born for the sacrifice, from the metres, is now born of the Brāhmaņa. First, he indeed is born of mucus, wherefore, who knows it even though the evil spirits contact (copulate with) a woman? And then, now indeed, he who is born of the sacrifice, of the metres, is born of ‘Brahmana’. Therefore, when even though one other than a Brāhmaņa, whether he be a Kṣatriya (Rājanya) or a Vaiśya, undertakes the vow (gets consecrated), they say of him only Brahmin. For, now indeed he is born of ‘Brāhmaņa’.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा᳘न्ह्येष᳘ उपाव᳘र्तते यो दी᳘क्षते स᳘ देव᳘तानामेवै᳘को भवत्य᳘थ यदा᳘ह ब्राह्मण इ᳘त्येत᳘र्हि वा᳘ एष ब्र᳘ह्मणो जायते यो᳘ यज्ञाच्छ᳘न्दोभ्यः श्लेष्म᳘णो वा ह्य᳘ग्रे जा᳘यते य᳘स्माद्वा को हि तद्वे᳘द यद्य᳘पि स्त्रि᳘यꣳ र᳘क्षाꣳस्यभिस᳘चन्ते᳘ऽथैत᳘र्हि ब्र᳘ह्मणो जा᳘यते यो᳘ यज्ञाच्छ᳘न्दोभ्यस्त᳘स्माद्य᳘द्यप्य᳘ब्राह्मणो दी᳘क्षते राज᳘न्यो वा वै᳘श्यो वा ब्राह्मण इत्ये᳘वैनमाहुरेत᳘र्हि हि ब्र᳘ह्मणो जा᳘यते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्मादाहुर्न᳓ सवनकृ᳓द्धन्त᳓व्य इ᳓त्येनस्व्ये᳙व᳓ सवनकृ᳓ता᳓तो हि᳓ स᳓ जा᳓यत इ᳓ति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore they say, “One who performs ‘Savana’ (the pressing of Soma/the Somasacrifice) should not be killed. For, because of the performer of the Soma sacrifice, he (the killer) verily becomes a sinner thereby. (First Brāhmaņa Ends)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्मादाहुर्न᳘ सवनकृ᳘द्धन्त᳘व्य इ᳘त्येन᳘स्व्येव᳘ सवनकृता᳘तो हि स जा᳘यत इ᳘ति ॥२७॥
</details>
